Faculty
Over the years,
Karachi Institute of Economics & Technology – KIET
has grown from strength to strength. Through its diverse
faculty & departments
, over 20+
unique programs
are offered for Bachelors, Masters, and Doctoral degrees across the disciplines of Business, Science , and Technology, fostering academic excellence and research-driven education.
Faculty of Computing & Information sciences
Cutting-edge programs in Artificial Intelligence , Computer Science, Cybersecurity, and Software Engineering and Enterprise Resource Planning.
Faculty of College of Management Sciences
MBA, BBA, and specialized programs in Business Intelligence, Accounting & Finance, Digital Marketing, and Aviation Management.
Why you should Join KIET?
We provide world-class education, state-of-the-art facilities, and a supportive learning environment to help students achieve their full potential. Our dedicated faculty and industry-relevant curriculum ensure students gain practical skills and knowledge for successful careers in a rapidly evolving global landscape. Strong collaborations with leading companies, allowing
students to participate.
Research & Innovation
Become a part of research culture that has already
generated a funding of over Rs. 200 million.
Insurance
Free student health insurance and life insurance of their parents.
scholarships and Concessions
A wide range of scholarships and concessions are available for students.
Experience Faculty
Highly qualified PhD and industry oriented faculty available for quality teaching.
Global Community
Join a global community of 13,000+ alumni working across the globe to excel in life.

1. Introduction
1.1 Scope of this Document
This document provides the description of BE (Electrical) program offered by College of Engineering
at Karachi Institute of Economics and Technology. This includes the program introduction, program
structure, scheme of studies and description of courses taught in the program.
1.2 Program Objectives
The objectives of BE (Electrical Engineering) program are to prepare electrical engineers:
1. With a sound knowledge of principles of mathematics, science, fundamentals of engineering design and
analysis, and professional ethics
2. Who are able to work in a global and diversified society
3. Who meet the needs of industry for electrical engineering or to pursue graduate studies.
4. Who are able to communicate clearly in written and oral formats.
5.
1.3 Program Composition
Electrical Engineering Department has come up with a comprehensive curriculum covering not only the
areas of engineering and sciences but also incorporating courses in humanities, social sciences and
management sciences. This is because Dept of EE wants to make visionary engineers with high ethical and
moral values working to serve the humanity.
BE (Electrical) is a 137 credit hours program. Courses have been classified as non-engineering and
engineering courses as shown in the table below.
Number of Courses Credit Hours
Non – Engineering 15 41
Engineering 29*/30 96
Total 43*/44 137
* For specialization in computer engineering
1
The curriculum has been designed to provide the student basic knowledge in the broader area of electrical
engineering. The course of study is focused on the fundamental concepts enabling the students to pursue a
career in any area from research and development to sales. In addition to the laboratory work that get the
student familiar with electrical, electronic and computing equipment and with experimental techniques,
course projects in almost every course help the student to learn how theoretical knowledge can be converted
into working projects.
The curriculum is designed in a way that the first three to four semesters cover the courses of general interest
(Engineering Foundation). This is followed by covering the electrical engineering breadth in the third year.
The final year include elective courses allowing the student to specialize either in Electronic Engineering,
Telecommunication Engineering or Computer Systems Engineering. A two-semester senior design project
usually involving industrial collaboration provides students with valuable experience in applying their
knowledge and skills for solution of real-world problems.
2

>>> OCR: Mechatronics.pdf <<<
Introduction
1
1.1 Vision Statement:
“We envision to be a center of excellence in Mechatronics engineering where highest quality
education produces ethical and visionary professionals at national and global levels with a focus
on research and entrepreneurship”.
1.2 Mission Statement:
“To empower future leaders in Mechatronics Engineering by equipping students with the technical,
teamwork, and entrepreneurial skills needed to excel in industry and academia”.
1.3 Program Educational Objectives
The objectives of BE (Mechatronic Engineering) program is to prepare Mechatronic engineers
1. Apply engineering knowledge and problem-solving skills to solve real-world challenges in
mechatronics, potentially pursuing entrepreneurial ventures
2. Design, develop, and improve innovative mechatronic systems while considering societal,
environmental and ethical aspects to drive industry advancement
3. Continuously analyze and investigate complex mechatronic problems using modern
engineering tools
4. Lead and collaborate effectively in teams through strong communication skills
5. Uphold a culture of lifelong learning for continuous adaptation in mechatronics
1.4 Program Composition
CoE has come up with a comprehensive curriculum covering not only the areas of engineering and
sciences but also incorporating courses in humanities, social sciences and management sciences.
This is because CoE wants to make visionary engineers with high ethical and moral values working
to serve humanity.
Starting Fall 2023, BE (Mechatronic) is a 136 credit hours program. Courses have been classified
as non-engineering and engineering courses as shown in the table below.

P age 3 | 647
The curriculum has been designed to provide the student basic knowledge in the broad area of
Mechatronic engineering. The course of study is focused on the fundamental concepts enabling
the students to pursue a career in any area from research and development to sales. In addition to
the laboratory work that makes the student familiar with electrical, electronic and computing
equipment and with experimental techniques, course projects, in almost every course, help the
student to learn how theoretical knowledge can be converted into working projects.
The curriculum is designed in a way that the first three to four semesters cover the courses of
general interest (Engineering Foundation). This is followed by covering the electrical engineering
breadth in the third year. The final year includes elective courses allowing the student to specialize
Mechatronic Engineering. A two-semester senior design project usually involving industrial
collaboration provides students with valuable experience in applying their knowledge and skills
for solution of real-world problems.

>>> OCR: SoftwareEngineering.pdf <<<
1. Introduction
1.1. Scope of this Document
This document provides the description of the BE (Software Engineering) program offered by the
College of Engineering at Karachi Institute of Economics and Technology. This includes the program
introduction, program structure, scheme of studies and description of courses taught in the program.
1.2. Vision of Software Engineering Program
The vision statement of the Software Engineering program is as follows
“To impart highest quality engineering education with an aim to produce proficient, creative and
ethical software engineers who can take the role of visionary leaders, entrepreneurs and researchers at
national and global levels”
The above vision was approved by the ACM held on 18th March, 2019
1.3. Mission of Software Engineering Program
The mission statement of the Software Engineering program is as follows
“To educate students through highly qualified faculty to have strong theoretical and practical
expertise, team-work, leadership qualities, ethical values and entrepreneurship skills. Establish and
maintain state-of-the-art laboratories, latest curriculum and industrial linkages. Prepare graduates to
pursue higher studies and undertake research at national and global levels. ”
The above mission was approved by the ACM held on 18th March, 2019
1.4. Program Education Objectives (PEOs)
There are three Program Education objectives of BE (Software Engineering) program:
1. PEO1: The graduates will be able to apply software engineering knowledge to critically
analyze, investigate and design innovative solutions for complex technological problems.
2. PEO2: The graduates will be able to demonstrate sustained career development through life
long learning, ethical practices and effective communication skills.
3. PEO3: The graduates will be able to use their skills by undertaking roles as entrepreneurs and
project managers to undertake new technological projects for development of society.
1.5. Program Composition BE Software Engineering
College of Engineering (CoE) has come up with a comprehensive curriculum covering not only the
areas of engineering and sciences but also incorporating courses in humanities, social sciences and
1
management sciences. This is because CoE wants to make visionary engineers with high ethical and
moral values working to serve the humanity.
BE (Software Engineering) is a 136 credit hours program. Courses have been classified as
General/Supporting and Computing/Software engineering courses as shown in the table below.
Number of Courses Credit Hours
General/Supporting 16 40
Computing 31 96
Total 47 136
Number of Courses Credit Hours
Humanities 4 10
Social Sciences 2 2
Management Sciences 3 5
Natural Sciences 7 23
Computing 2 6
Engineering Foundation 9 32
Core Breath of Engineering 9 27
Core Depth of Engineering 6 18
Multi-disciplinary 3 7
Final year project 2 6
Total 47 136
The curriculum has been designed to provide the student basic knowledge in the broader area of
computing and software engineering. The course of study is focused on the fundamental concepts
enabling the students to pursue a career in any area from research and development to sales. In
addition to the laboratory work that get the student familiar with computing, software engineering and
electronics related equipment and with experimental techniques, course projects in almost every
course help the student to learn how theoretical knowledge can be converted into working projects.
2
A two-semester senior design project usually involving industrial collaboration provides students with
valuable experience in applying their knowledge and skills for solution of real-world problems.
3

>>> OCR: Avionics.pdf <<<
1. Scope of this Document
This document provides the description of BE (Avionics) program offered by College of Engineering at
Karachi Institute of Economics and Technology. This includes the program introduction, program
structure, scheme of studies and description of courses taught in the program.
2. Introduction
Program Objectives
The objectives of BE (Avionic Engineering) program are to prepare avionic engineers:
1. With a sound knowledge of principles of mathematics, science, fundamentals of engineering design
and analysis, and professional ethics
2. Who are able to work in a global and diversified society
3. Who meet the needs of industry for Avionic Engineering or to pursue graduate studies?
4. And who are also able to communicate clearly in written and oral formats.
Program Composition
CoE has come up with a comprehensive curriculum covering not only the areas of engineering and sciences
but also incorporating courses in humanities, social sciences and management sciences. This is because
CoE wants to make visionary engineers with high ethical and moral values working to serve the humanity.
BE (Avionics) is a 134 credit hours program excluding the 6 credit hours of Sports and Internships. Courses
have been classified as non-engineering and engineering courses as shown in the table below.
Number of Courses Credit Hours
Non – Engineering 16 41
Engineering 30 93
Total 46 134
1
The curriculum has been designed to provide the student basic knowledge in the broader area of Electrical
Engineering which is followed by focused courses related to Avionic Engineering. The course of study is
focused on the fundamental concepts enabling the students to pursue a career in any area from research
and development to sales. In addition to the laboratory work that get the student familiar with electrical,
electronic and computing equipment and with experimental techniques, course projects in almost every
course help the student to learn how theoretical knowledge can be converted into working projects.
The curriculum is designed in a way that the first three to four semesters cover the courses of general
interest (Engineering Foundation). This is followed by covering the Avionic Engineering breadth starting
from the third year and extending into fifth and sixth semesters. The senior semesters from fifth till eighth
semester include depth courses allowing the student to specialize in Avionic Engineering. A two-semester
senior design project usually involving industrial collaboration provides students with valuable experience
in applying their knowledge and skills for solution of real-world problems.
2

About the Faculty of Computer Science
The Faculty of Computer Science at KIET offers a dynamic learning environment focused on innovation, research, and real-world impact. With a strong foundation in theory and hands-on practice, we prepare students for the future of technology through state-of-the-art labs, industry-driven curriculum, and experienced faculty.
Academic Programs
BS Computer Science
Accredited by NCEAC, designed with market-driven skills
MS Computer Science
Research-focused with specializations in AI, Networks, and Data Science
PhD Computer Science
Advanced research for future academicians and scientists
Message
Welcome to the Faculty of Computing & Information Sciences (FoCIS) at KIET. As President of this esteemed institution, I am proud to lead a dynamic academic environment where innovation, research, and excellence converge. At CoCIS, we are committed to equipping our students with the knowledge, skills, and ethical grounding necessary to thrive in the rapidly evolving digital world. With a focus on emerging technologies such as Artificial Intelligence, Cybersecurity, Data Science, and Software Engineering, our programs are designed to meet global standards while addressing local challenges. As a PhD scholar and lifelong advocate for quality education, I believe in nurturing not just competent professionals, but visionary leaders who will shape the future of technology in Pakistan and beyond. I invite you to explore the opportunities that await you at FoCIS and become a part of our journey towards academic and technological excellence.
Dr. Khalid Khan
President, KIET
Meet Our Faculty
Our faculty includes PhD scholars and industry veterans with expertise in Machine Learning, Data Analytics, Cloud Computing, and more. Their mentorship transforms students into professionals ready to lead.
The strength of the Faculty of Computing & Information Sciences (FoCIS) lies in its distinguished and diverse faculty. Our team comprises experienced professors, PhD scholars, and industry professionals dedicated to academic excellence, cutting-edge research, and student mentorship. With expertise in fields such as Artificial Intelligence, Cybersecurity, Cloud Computing, Software Engineering, and Data Science, our faculty members bring real-world relevance to the classroom and foster a culture of innovation, ethics, and collaboration.
“At FoCIS, we’re not just teaching the tools of today — we’re inspiring students to build the technology of tomorrow.”
Permanent Faculty
Adjunct Faculty

About CoMS
The Faculty of Management Sciences (CoMS) at KIET offers dynamic business education with an entrepreneurial mindset. We prepare students for leadership roles in corporate, public, and startup environments.
With a strong focus on innovation, ethics, and industry relevance, CoMS equips students with practical skills and strategic thinking. Our faculty combines academic expertise with real-world experience to deliver impactful learning. Through research, case studies, and hands-on projects, we ensure students are future-ready and globally competitive.
Explore
Our Academic Programs
CoMS offers HEC-approved undergraduate, graduate, and doctoral programs designed to meet market needs.
Undergraduate Programs
A 4-year undergraduate program designed to build strong foundations in business, leadership, and innovation. Students can choose single or dual majors to align with their career goals..
GRADUATE PROGRAMS
These flexible programs are tailored for early to mid-career professionals aiming to enhance managerial and strategic decision-making skills. Weekend and evening options are available for working executives.
Associate Degree in MS
A research-oriented graduate degree focused on advanced business theories and analytical skills. Ideal for those pursuing academic, consulting, or policy-making careers.
Specialization
A rigorous doctoral program that cultivates high-level research capabilities and thought leadership. Candidates contribute original research to the fields of management, marketing, finance, and more.
Explore
Explore
Explore
Explore
Vision & Mission
Shaping ethical, innovative, and industry-ready leaders through excellence in business education and research.
vision
To become a leading institution of business education and research in Pakistan, known for academic rigor, industry relevance, and global competitiveness.
MISSION
To develop future-ready professionals who embody innovation, integrity, and leadership. We aim to deliver high-impact education through a curriculum rooted in real-world practices, supported by research, entrepreneurship, and meaningful industry engagement.
Research & Publications
The College of Management Sciences (CoMS) is dedicated to advancing knowledge through quality research, faculty development, and student engagement.
Our flagship journal, Market Forces, serves as a platform for cutting-edge research in management, economics, and business.
Explore
IRCBM – International Conference
The International Research Conference on Business & Management (IRCBM) connects academia with industry and showcases impactful research from across the globe. It provides a platform for scholars, practitioners, and students to exchange ideas, present innovations, and foster global collaborations.
Explore
Meet Our Faculty
Our faculty comprises experienced educators and researchers with national and international credentials.
They mentor students in academics, research, and career paths, fostering critical thinking and leadership.
With a strong commitment to innovation and industry relevance, they ensure our programs remain future-focused and impactful.
Their diverse expertise and industry engagement provide students with practical knowledge and global business perspectives.
Explore

WELCOME
TO THE
COLLEGE OF MANAGEMENT SCIENCES
We are inspired by Joseph Schumpeter‟s characterization of the entrepreneur as a heroic figure, a creative
destroyer whose characteristic task is the breaking up and the creation of new traditions. Our mission is to
invigorate our faculty and our students with the entrepreneurial spirit necessary for breaking up old traditions and
the creation of new ones. Continuous innovation and change is our motto. We are continually striving to improve
and develop. Our business programs now have the distinction of taking the lead in incorporating the
“entrepreneurship experience” as an integral part of the curriculum. Students joining the business degree programs,
especially BBA and MBA programs automatically become part of the process that provides the “entrepreneurship
experience” through a business startup in groups. Students have the opportunity to put their creativity to work
through the assigned project work, making students apply theoretical knowledge they gather during the class
lectures Strategically designed courses in successive semesters motivate the students and help them in conceiving
the idea, planning, financing, marketing and managing the startup business venture. Each group that exhibits the
potential, is assigned a project to setup an enterprise during their stay at the Institute.
We expect that our students will work as a team to build the reputation of the institution and the College of
Management Sciences.
Good Luck!
Faculty and Staff
College of Management Sciences
KIET
VISION STATEMENT OF COLLEGE OF MANAGEMENT SCIENCES
Our vision is to be one of the leading institutions in the higher business education by employing creative measures
to build students‟ skills and capacities.
MISSION STATEMENT OF COLLEGE OF MANAGEMENT SCIENCES
Our mission is to invigorate our faculty and our students with the entrepreneurial spirit necessary for breaking up
of old traditions and the creation of new ones. Continuous innovation and change is our motto. We are continuously
striving to improve and develop. We are willing to experiment and innovate; we are ready to take risks and manage
the results of our actions.
Page 3 of 14
Academic Department
Academic Department o Director College of Management Sciences manages the overall execution of the
programs of morning and evening/weekend --- academically and administratively that are offered at the Main
Campus and the city centers that are the extension of the Main Campus. The Director of the College of
Management Sciences (CoMS) is supported by the five Head of Departments (Area Specialists), namely HoD
Marketing, HoD Finance and HoD HRM, HoD Management and HoD Economics for academic issues and
faculty selection. The faculty, academic staff and other staff also support in the academic and administrative
work.
o Director CoMS also supports various other functions, like education seminars and project exhibitions,
management of the student societies.
o Students studying in various programs are advised to maintain a close contact with the respective designated
Counselors and Program Managers for academic counseling and administrative issues.
o The College of Management Sciences has resolution of an open door policy for all students giving them
access to the College Director, when required, for any academic or administrative issues. However, the
Counselors and Program Managers should also be aware of the issues being forwarded to the Director by the
staff or by the students themselves.
o Permanent Faculty members have designated time slots for students where they are available without any
prior appointment for students queries.
o Program Manager and other academic staff (officers) are available at each center/campus to help students
with their queries. The College believes in the open door policy where students are not required to take prior
approval to meet an academic staff member for guidance and consultation.
Recognized Degree
• KIET received recognition by the Higher Education Commission (formerly named as UGC) vide letter
no. 15-22/UGC• SEC/97/1219/ dated August 1, 1998. KIET was awarded a degree granted status through
a charter from Government of Sindh (CIEC) on May 24, 2000.
• Accreditation received from National Business Education Accreditation Council (NBEAC).
• High Academic Quality Rating of College of Management Sciences by Sindh Govt.(CIEC).
Other Departments of the Institute
Finance and Accounts Services
All payments are made in the Finance and Accounts Department (FAD). A student window provides assistance
and responds to all queries.
Registration & Examination Department
Office of the Registration & Examination handles changes in registration, name and address changes, grade reports,
and transcripts. The office also enrolls students at the institute, issues degrees and grade transcripts.
Library at the Institute
Central library houses more than 16000 books and subscribes to over 50 journals. Students can borrow materials
from the library with their student library cards. In addition, the College of Management Sciences (CoMS)
subscribed to international database called EBSCO for faculty and students to accommodate research related
activities.
Corporate Services and Industrial Liaison
The College of Management Sciences arranges and accommodates services like:
• Event management (conferences, seminars, exhibitions),
• Support Guest speakers support if requested by Colleges,
Page 4 of 14
• Help students directly in writing resumes, covering letters and improve interview skills. (Part of the
curriculum also)
In addition, the department of Corporate Relations provides services and receives support from the College (CoMS)
for:
• Career counseling, placement assistance, internship assignments,
Management Information Systems Department (MIS)
Maintains the Institute‟s databases. It offers all students the opportunity to get user ID and password. With the
database access, students are facilitated in self-registration, updating their personal data, viewing their attendance
status, results etc.
Admissions Office
The admission office is responsible for admission-related issues. It also evaluates and allows transfer of academic
credits of the enrolled students, whether obtained in the other colleges of KIET or other universities.
KIET Students Societies in Management Sciences
The institute has a large number of extracurricular available for students. This includes becoming members of
professional associations / societies in various academic departments and outside. It supports an array of social,
educational and cultural activities on the campus.
Academic System
The Semester System
The academic year at College of Management Sciences is composed of two regular semesters of 16 weeks. In
addition, there is a summer semester of about 8 weeks, offered to students following HEC (Higher Education
Commission) conditions.
Academic Counseling
Teachers, especially HODs and counselors, may be considered as mentors who may be contacted to plan specific
program of studies. However, designated academic counselors from the faculty body are assigned for providing
counseling to students in every semester.
Faculty Board
1. A Faculty Board comprising Dean Academics, Director CoMS, and respective HODs (Area Specialists)
is held regularly in every semester (Fall and Spring) to take decisions on Academic related issues
2. Students continuously failing to avoid conditions for ‘Probation’ (GPA less than 2.00) shall appear
before the members of the Faculty Board.
3. Student with CGPA less than 2.00 at the end of 4th semester can be considered for Academic
Dismissal by the Faculty Board.
Page 5 of 14
Academic Probation and Dismissal
A student must maintain a minimum GPA or CGPA of 2.00 to avoid ‘Probation’. Poor grades may lead to eventual
dismissal.
Grade Point Average (GPA) &Cumulative Grade Point Average (CGPA)
Grade Point Average (GPA) is calculated on the basis of course load in a semester for which a letter grade is given,
whereas, Cumulative Grade Point Average (CGPA) is calculated on the basis of all coursework undertaken at KIET
for which also a letter grade is given. (The symbols R and W are not considered in calculating the grade point
average). Academic Warning
GPA for ‘Academic Warning’ Student Status
Less than 2.0 Watch list
Academic Probation
GPA for ‘Academic Probation’ Student Status
Less than 2.0 after warning received Probation
Academic Dismissal
CGPA for ‘Academic Dismissal’ Student Status
Less than 2.00 at the end of 4th semester Academic Dismissal
Restrictions on Students having ‘Academic Probation’:
1. Students on Academic Probation cannot take more than 09 credit hours course load without the
permission from the Dean and the Director College of Management Sciences.
2. Students on Academic Probation are not eligible for registration through Self-Registration System
(SRS).
3. Student on Academic Probation cannot hold office in any society or organization; represent KIET at
any official or social event without permission of the Director College of Management
Sciences
Academic System
Course Registration
Students should preferably seek registration in the particular set of courses offered by the Department through the
Self Registration System (SRS). Any deviation has to be approved by the students Counselors in advance.
Course Numbering System
All courses are usually identified by three letters followed by three digits (numbers). The letters are abbreviation
of the area and the number identifies the seniority of course.
List of these abbreviations is as follows:
ACT: Accounting
COM: Communication
ECO: Economics
FIN: Finance
MAN: Management
MKT: Marketing
QT: Quantitative SS:
Social Sciences
Page 6 of 14
Marks Distribution for Grading
Teachers at the Institute generally use fixed grading scale with the maximum allocation of 100 points representing
100% for a course. Generally midterm is assigned 25% points, final examination that is comprehensive is assigned
40% points. A group oriented industry based projects in the core business courses are assigned 25 points including
the presentation points in front of the faculty member or the external evaluator from the industry or both.
Assignments/quizzes/in-class activities are given 10% points. A faculty member has the right to modify this
grading scheme but with prior approval from HoD (Area Specialist) and the Director of the College.
Grading System
The letter grade assigned in a course has a numeric equivalent:
Grade Grade
Points
A 4 Superior
B+ 3.5 Very Good
B 3 Good
C+ 2.5 Average
C 2 Below Average
F 0 Fail
A student must earn an "A", “B+ “ or a "B" in order to have what are generally considered "good grades." If a
student earns only a “C+" or a "C" his grade is considered marginal and his grade point average (GPA) is poor. .
KIET follows Absolute Grading System with maximum GPA 4.00. Following is the grading scheme:
4.0 A 87 – 100
3.5 B+ 78 – 86
3.0 B 72 – 77
2.5 C+ 66 – 71
2.0 C 60 – 65
(Fail) F Less then-60
Grade Point Average (GPA)
A student's grade-point average (GPA) is calculated by dividing the number of credits earned into the number of
grade points earned in a semester. The "cumulative GPA" (CGPA) is the GPA a student has earned for all courses
undertaken over the entire duration of a program of studies.
General Information
„Course Outline Sheet‟, is provided to students in the first week. Course outline has the course name on the
top and highlights the course objectives, topics to be covered in each class session, the main text book,
reference books if any that is recommended for the course and by the faculty, and grading scheme. Students
are also provided „Student Assessment Information Sheet‟ to assess the class and faculty during the semester
and the information is recorded in the database for consultation and record.
Students are encouraged to participate in the class. Instructors promote and support discussion making them
overcome shyness to express themselves verbally. The Institute has the policy of enforcing the students‟
overall preparedness. It is enforced through pop quizzes, tests and class participation.
Page 7 of 14
Instructional Methods
Teaching Components:
a) Lectures
b) Tutorials / In-Class Exercises
c) Discussion on Case Studies
d) Seminars and Presentations
e) Laboratory Work
f) Course Project
g) Term Papers
The Institute encourages a policy of flexibility in methods of instructions. Teachers use white boards,
multimedia or class discussion to communicate.
„Lecture Plan/Course Outline‟ is prepared meticulously and includes session wise breakup spread over
specified sessions.
The number and marks distribution of Mid-Term Tests, Quizzes, Assignments, Course Project, Term Paper
and Final Examination etc are clearly specified in „Course Outline” as per policy and are given to the students.
i) Assignments, projects and presentations are frequently given and students are encouraged to refer to reference
books, journals and web resources. The original work of the students is encouraged. ii) Course projects are assigned
to maintain a balance in students‟ workload in the entire semester.
iii) Generally, the book (s) referred as textbook for the course is affordable and available in the market.
iv) Students are exposed to the provisional result of at least marks (50%) before the final exam. All assessed
components (except the final exam) are graded and displayed on the notice board before the start of final
exams. The system is transparent and precludes correction at a later stage.
v) In order to develop students‟ communication skills in English, it has been declared as the official language of
communication during lecturing, classroom activities and all other formal and informal occasions.
Attendance Rules
Attendance rule is strictly enforced. Class attendance is normally taken within the first 15 minutes of the session.
Students shall be dropped from the course and will not be allowed to appear in the final exams if the absences in a
semester exceed 20% of the lectures planned for the course. After drop from the course, a student will not be
allowed to sit in the class and the registration in that course shall be canceled.

Page 8 of 14
Research Journal
Market Forces is an academic research journal published by the College of Management Sciences. This is an open
source journal which is internationally indexed by the Directory of Open Access Journals {DOAJ), Sweden. The
journal is in its tenth year of publication and is published bi-annually. Market Forces has an international advisory
board with academics and professionals both from home and aboard. The contents of the journal include research
papers, case studies, book reviews, policy papers and conference reports. Market Forces has received wide acclaim
from academic and research circles in Pakistan and internationally.
Examinations
General Information
a. Midterm: Midterm and Final examination will be held in all semesters.
b. Duration of Exams: Midterm (2 hours) / Final (3 hrs)
c. Marks: 25 marks (midterm)/ 40 marks (Final).
d. Type of paper: As per policy guidelines.
e. Schedules: Midterm and final examination shall be held on announced dates. Normally one midterm exam
/ one final exam paper will be scheduled on a day.
f. Makeup: There shall be no make-up exam for any assessed component except for the policy prescribed
by the Examination Department.
Distribution of Marks: In accordance with the general grading scheme described in the previous section and
provided on the course outline for each student in a course. It is the policy of the institute that the marks at the
discretion of the teacher are kept to a minimum and the marks obtained by the students reflect their actual academic
performances.
Note:
The results of all quizzes/ assignments/ exams are entered into the database (MIS).
All quizzes, assignments are given back to the students after checking and midterm exams are discussed
between faculty and the student in the classroom. Final exam can be asked for scrutiny by a student if
unsatisfied by the final exam‟s grading by the faculty. If asked for scrutiny, the final exam will be rechecked
by another faculty and the marks of the scrutinized paper shall be considered for the final grade.
Academic Dishonesty
Academic dishonesty includes cheating, plagiarism, or collusion. Any or all these methods may result in
serious penalties and even academic dismissal from the Institute.
(Cheating means getting help that a student is not supposed to get on an assignment, quiz, or examination.
Plagiarism refers specifically to the practice of copying from a book or other publication and not acknowledging
the material used. Collusion is collaborating with another person without proper authorization when preparing an
assignment).
Use of Unfair Means: Cheating/Plagiarism
Use of unfair means is considered a very serious offense. Students can be rusticated, suspended, demoted
or expelled from the institute.
Cases of cheating or plagiarism are referred to Examination Disciplinary Committee that investigates the
matter and recommend necessary action(s) to President KIET.
Carrying of mobile phones and any other communication device is strictly prohibited in the
examination rooms and, if found, same shall be confiscated
No student shall be allowed to sit in the examination, 10- minutes after start of examination.
No student shall be allowed to leave examinations room, earlier than 40-minutes in the midterm exam and
60 minutes in the final examination after the commencement of the paper.
Students may consult the Plagiarism policy for detail policy.
Page 9 of 14
Keys to Academic Success
Understand the System
• The semester academic system differs from all others systems. To succeed in it, you need to learn how it
is organized and how it works!
• In the semester system, the best student is the one with the highest cumulative grade point average.
• Academic success in the semester system requires consistent, disciplined study for all projects,
assignments, examinations, and courses.
• Period of adjustment to a new educational system may be necessary before you are able to perform to the
best of your ability.
• The lesser the time taken to understand the system the better the chance of good CGPA.
Course selection
• Add / Drop Procedures: You should be familiar with the "pass-fail" grades and the procedures for
dropping and adding courses
• Number of Courses: Divide your effort equally and time according to your abilities and understanding
of a course.
• Consulting: Do not hesitate to consult faculty and academic staff on academic matters.
Work hard from the beginning
• Don’t Wait Till The End: It is generally not possible in the semester system to wait till the end of the
semester to study
• No Respite Throughout the Semester: If you do not begin studying on the first day of classes, you are
likely to get behind and experience academic difficulty as grades are based on a series of tests, papers,
presentations, and projects instead of one final exam, a system you may have been accustomed previously.
Interact with the teachers
• Teachers expect students to ask questions in class or immediately following class
• Most teachers appreciate students who raise questions or challenges in the class, showing interest in the
subject.
Ask Questions
• If a student feels unsure of what is expected to have been learnt in the class, ask the teacher. If you do not
ask, it will be assumed that you understand everything or that you are not interested
Keys to Academic Success
Study Strategies, Motivation, and Time Management
• Remember that the semester system rewards students who can:
– study a large amount of material
concerning a broad range of subjects
– synthesize material from many sources –
take examinations effectively
Page 10 of 14
These activities require skills that can be learned
Study Skills
 Organizing Your Time
– You will have a large amount of work to do in a limited amount of time. In this situation, you
need to use your time effectively
– A good way to do that is to make a weekly study schedule. Allocate specified periods of time
each day for studying
Note: A general rule is that the student will need to spend at least two hours studying for each one hour
spent in class
Reading Effectively
• Looking at the length of the reading lists, you will realize that it is not possible to memorize all of your
reading materials for the semester, or even to study them in reasonable depth.
• You are expected to familiarize yourself only with the main points from each reading and be able to relate
what one writer has said to what another writer has said on the same matter.
• To draw main points from a large number of readings, here are some things you can do:
o Skim - "Skimming" means looking over a reading
quickly o Read - Go over the material again
o Questions - Rather than passively accepting what the
writer has written, ask yourself questions o Review - Skim it
again. Look at your notes again. Try to retain the main points
Deriving as Much as Possible from Classes
• Attendance and participation in classes is an important part of the semester system
– Read in advance
– Take notes
– Review
– Do not be discouraged
– Keep trying
Keys to Academic Success
Coping with Quizzes and Examinations
• Keep up to date on your studies
• Schedule time to review
• Rest before the test
• Read test instructions carefully
• Schedule your time
Credits
• The quantity of academic work a student does in semester system is measured in "credits"
• The number of credits depends on the number of hours per week a course has
• The student who has achieved a passing grade in a course has earned three/ two/ one credits.
• In order to graduate a student must earn a specified number of credits and a minimum prescribed CGPA.
Page 11 of 14
Grade Point Average (GPA)
• GPA Calculation: The grade-point average (GPA) is calculated by dividing the number of credits earned
into the number of grade points earned
• GPA Deficiency: A student has a grade point deficiency when the total number of grade points earned is
less than twice the total number of semester hours attempted Time Management Strategy
• Task-by-Task: Works on tasks in order of priority and as time is available
• Goal-setting: A task is broken into sections each having a specific deadline
• Agenda: An organized plan of tasks needing completion, a to do list Important
• Students have to realize that differences exist between the semester and other educational systems
• A student will have to adjust his/her thinking if he/she is going to succeed academically
• Whether or not a student personally accepts the values of semester system, he/she will have to act in
accordance with them while studying in a semester system
Graduation Requirements
On meeting the following requirements, a student becomes eligible for the award of degree:
• Earned the total number of credit hours specified for the degree program
• Earned required number of credit hours from different departments or fields of study
• Successful completion of internships
• Success in comprehensive examination (conducted on completion of coursework).
• On completion of coursework, minimum CGPA 2.00 for Bachelors and 2.5 CGPA for the Masters degree
programs
Academic Planning for Professional Life
Academic Work
Take additional courses in management, social sciences, and inter-disciplinary subjects if you can possibly do so.
Very frequently, people are placed into administrative positions for which they generally lack academic
preparation. Try to anticipate that learn the methods which help you undertake inter-disciplinary topics.
Personal Orientation
Have clear goals for your studies and other activities at College of Management Sciences. The goals might include
the idea of preparing for a variety of types of jobs. Cultivate patience and non-judgmental attitude; these two
personal characteristics that will help you interact constructively with people whose ideas are different from yours.
Extracurricular Activities
Get leadership and management experience through student organizations, professional associations, participation
in voluntary service activities. Improve your English. Those who use English well have better opportunities for
professional advancement.
Profession-Related Activities
Get practical experience in your field. Through volunteer work, arranged internship, get as much practical
experience as you can. Most prospective employers want someone who has practical experience as well as a
theoretical understanding of a subject.
Join and participate in professional organizations, on the campus and elsewhere. Ask in your departmental office
about professional organizations in your field where students can join.
Attend professional conferences. Seek help of Corporate Service and Industrial Liaison Office.
Establish relationships with fellow professionals. Get acquainted with people in your field of study. Establish
relationships "networking" with people who can help you in your professional life.
Build a personal library of books and handbooks that can help you once you graduate and are in employment.
Page 12 of 14
Student Services
Office of the Director of the College
 Manages academic support to the students
 Manages current semester and plans for next semester
 Coordination with registrar‟s office on academic matters
 Coordination for Faculty, Staff, GSSE, Student
Societies/Clubs, other colleges
Office of the Manager Student Support Services
 Conducts Faculty Board every semester
 Accomplishes following tasks in respect of students with CGPA 2.20 and below:
• Monitor performance
• Determine course load
• Registration
• Monitor class absences
• Maintain performance log
• Maintain close liaison with the parents
Help handle and resolve student issues
Financial Support
Financial support is available to senior students (with superior academic performance) in the form of grader or
teaching assistant. A student can apply through your departmental academic office if you want an assistantship.
Part-Time Employment
The office of the Director must approve any on-campus "student employment". To obtain such an approval, fill out
a "Request for Employment Authorization" form available with the Academic Support Office. Within a few days
(if an appropriate vacancy exist), a letter will be issued in a sealed envelope for you to take it to the HR department.
Students wishing to work off-campus must get prior permission for doing so.
Merit Scholarships
Students with ≥ 15credit hours of course load (except 1st semester students) and a strong academic record (i.e. GPA
≥ 3.5) are eligible for scholarships. The Institute funds these merit scholarships.
Transport Services
Pick and drop facility at nominal charges is available for the students. The transport services cover all major parts
of the city. A free shuttle service is also available between the Main Campus and Korangi Crossing at announced
times.
Page 13 of 14
ADDITIONAL SOURCES OF INFORMATION
Campus Publication
Institution Calendar: An important general resources related to academics. Published every semester, it
includes a planning calendar with a listing of academic events.
Schedule of Classes: A listing of specific course offerings and times they are taught in a given semester in all
the three centers (campuses).Published every semester, the spring /Summer/Fall Schedule of Classes for the
next semester is generally available near the end of current semester.
Prospectus: Academic and student service information relevant to students .It is published every year.
Electronic Reference Sources:
World Wide Web-KIET’s homepage on the Web (http://www.kiet.edu.pk/) can direct you to departmental
Websites as well as many other locations
Self-Registration System (SRS)
SRS is liberal database application window for students and the unique online provision for multiple
purposes/usages/facilities provided by MIS department. The web (http://mis.kiet.edu.pk/) can direct you to
access MIS Portal through Database URLs for student’s login to:
 SRS student application window to register courses
 Students query box for any academic administrative issue
 View academic records.

QEC-Where Quality Meets Innovation
KIET’s QEC leads the way in academic quality assurance, embedding continuous improvement into every aspect of institutional growth.
Learn MORE
Cultivating Quality. Inspiring Excellence.
Shaping higher education through strategic planning, interdisciplinary research, and student-focused excellence.
Learn MORE
Accountability. Integrity. Impact.
Our commitment to transparent processes and national standards ensures academic delivery with real-world relevance.
Learn MORE
/ 3
/ 3
About KIET
Ensuring Excellence in Every Dimension
Vision and Mission
Vision:
To be a premier higher education institution of Pakistan, fostering a culture of innovation in teaching and research that bridges theory and practice – empowering students with cutting-edge skills, critical thinking, leadership traits, and entrepreneurial mindset to create a meaningful social impact.
Mission:
Empowering students to achieve excellence in their chosen fields, we commit to:
Delivering innovative and industry-relevant education.
Fostering a culture of critical thinking, creativity, and entrepreneurship.
Providing opportunities for experiential learning and practical application.
Building strong partnerships with industry, community, and alumni.
Developing graduates who are adaptable, innovative, and socially responsible, and nurturing a lifelong connection with our alumni to foster a community of leaders and change-makers.
Quality Assurance Policy
Our Commitment to Academic Integrity and Institutional Growth
Statement:
“KIET is committed to fostering a strong internal quality culture that emphasizes continuous improvement in academic delivery and institutional performance. Quality assurance is integrated into daily operations through a holistic and participatory approach, driven by all departments and overseen by the Quality Enhancement Cell (QEC).”
– In alignment with Policy Action 6.2, Page 41 – Quality Assurance Manual for Higher Education in Pakistan
KIET upholds academic integrity through a structured and transparent quality assurance framework.
Our commitment drives continuous institutional growth, innovation, and educational excellence.
Vision and Mission
Leading with Quality, Impacting with Innovation. Driving Continuous Improvement for a Transformative Education
Vision
"To institutionalize a sustainable culture of quality assurance and continuous quality improvement at KIET by aligning academic and administrative processes with HEC policies, national priorities, and international best practices in higher education."
Mission
“The Quality Enhancement Cell (QEC) at Karachi Institute of Economics and Technology (KIET) is committed to:• Implementing HEC’s Quality Assurance Frameworks through effective compliance with policies, standards, and guidelines. • Institutionalizing Continuous Quality Improvement (CQI) through systematic monitoring and evaluation. • Strengthening Outcome-Based Education (OBE) by supporting CLO and PLO assessment and improvement. • Enhancing teaching, learning, and assessment quality through evidence-based review mechanisms. • Ensuring transparency, accountability, and good governance through proper documentation and audits. • Facilitating faculty development and academic effectiveness. • Supporting accreditation, ranking, and benchmarking initiatives. • Aligning academic programs, research, and outreach with national priorities and Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs).”
Core Values
Academic Excellence
Strive for the highest standards in teaching, learning, and research.
Student-Centric Approach
Prioritize the holistic development of students to prepare them for leadership roles.
Innovation and Adaptability
Embrace emerging technologies and methodologies in both Business and Computer Science.
Ethical Integrity
Uphold transparency, fairness, and accountability in all processes.
Collaboration and Partnership
Build strong ties with industry, alumni, and academic networks.
Impact-Driven Research
Promote research that addresses societal needs and contributes to sustainable development.

Directorate of Advanced Studies & Research (DASR)
With a mission to be a research-led university, KIET has a rapidly developing and vibrant research ecosystem consisting of world-class researchers, facilities, stakeholder engagement, and administrative support system.
Explore
Read More
Our Mission & Vision
Elevating postgraduate studies through quality, innovation, and impact.
Established in Spring 2025, DASR serves as the central academic and administrative body overseeing all MS and PhD programs at KIET. We coordinate research, supervise academic standards, and promote multidisciplinary collaborations to position KIET as a hub for research excellence in Pakistan.
LATEST NEWS
PhD final defense of our scholar Mr. Mohammad Ayub Latif is scheduled to be held on 6 November 2025 at 11:00 AM in the Conference Room, NN Campus, or via Zoom.
PhD final defense of our scholar Mr. Zubair is scheduled to be held 25 September 2025 at 10 am NN campus.
Renaming of GSSE to DASR
Announcement of an Open Ph.D. Defense of Zeba Shariff Khan on July 26 2023
Announcement of an Open PhD Defense of Mr. Zubair Ahmed Shah on September 30, 2021.
Graduate Programs Supervision
Elevating postgraduate studies through quality, innovation, and impact.
DASR ensures quality in curricula, assessments, and research across all faculties. From course planning to thesis defenses, we provide transparent, well-structured academic pathways, enabling students to achieve their academic and professional goals.
Driving Knowledge Beyond Boundaries
Collaborative research for a better future.
We promote high-impact interdisciplinary research across fields like AI, sustainable development, healthcare, and cybersecurity. Our scholars and faculty are encouraged to publish, innovate, and contribute to national and international research landscapes.
Research Enterprise
KIET culture of collaboration drives innovative discoveries in areas vital to our world, our health and our intellectual life.
Interdisciplinary Research
At the intersection of disciplines is where new ideas emerge and innovative research happens.
Institutes, Labs & Centers
Independent labs, centers and institutes engage faculty and students from across the university.
Other research centers & labs
Academic departments sponsor numerous other research centers and labs.
Research Conferences
KIET organize individual research conferences specifically from Engineering, Management & Computing Sciences departments.
ICCIS-CoCIS
International Conference on Computing & Information Sciences
To establish the research culture among the students and faculty members, KIET sort out a global gathering, International Conference on Computing & Information Sciences (ICCIS). To produce quality professionals by using diverse learning methodology, aspiring faculty, innovative curriculum and cutting edge research, in the field of computing & information sciences
INTELLECT-CoE
International Conference On Electrical Engineering
The aim of this conference is to bring together researchers, scientists, academicians and representatives of industry to discuss the latest directions of research in engineering technologies, identify solutions to current national problems through industry-academia collaboration and to contribute in nation building.
IRCBM-CoMS
International Research Conferences on Business Management
The aim of IRCBM is to provide platform for educators, Researcher, Academicians, Scholars, Managers, Graduate students and Entrepreneurs from different culture backgrounds to present and discuss researches, development and innovation in the field of Business Management and Entrepreneurship.
Director’s Message
Inspiring research, fostering excellence.
At DASR, research is more than an academic requirement—it’s our commitment to society. Together, let’s advance knowledge, uphold integrity, and shape a future defined by innovation.” — Prof. Dr. Maaz Bin Ahmad
Read More
News & updates
Stay informed with the latest announcements, events, and opportunities from DASR KIET.
Connect with DASR
Partner with us in shaping the research leaders of tomorrow.
Whether you’re a prospective student, faculty member, or research partner, DASR welcomes collaboration and dialogue. Contact us to learn more about our programs, research support, and academic initiatives.
Email:-
dasr@kiet.edu.pk

Faculty
Over the years,
Karachi Institute of Economics & Technology – KIET
has grown from strength to strength. Through its diverse
faculty & departments
, over 20+
unique programs
are offered for Bachelors, Masters, and Doctoral degrees across the disciplines of Business, Science , and Technology, fostering academic excellence and research-driven education.
Faculty of Computing & Information sciences
Cutting-edge programs in Artificial Intelligence , Computer Science, Cybersecurity, and Software Engineering and Enterprise Resource Planning.
Faculty of College of Management Sciences
MBA, BBA, and specialized programs in Business Intelligence, Accounting & Finance, Digital Marketing, and Aviation Management.
Why you should Join KIET?
We provide world-class education, state-of-the-art facilities, and a supportive learning environment to help students achieve their full potential. Our dedicated faculty and industry-relevant curriculum ensure students gain practical skills and knowledge for successful careers in a rapidly evolving global landscape. Strong collaborations with leading companies, allowing
students to participate.
Research & Innovation
Become a part of research culture that has already
generated a funding of over Rs. 200 million.
Insurance

scholarships and Concessions

Explore Our Academic Programs
Degrees That Lead to Careers
KIET offers a rich variety of academic programs designed to meet the demands of today’s job market. Our computing and business programs are backed by specialized labs and research facilities, innovation, and leadership. Whether you aim to build software, design aircraft, manage a business, or produce digital content, KIET provides the structure, support, and mentorship you need. Faculty members with real-world experience ensure your learning goes beyond theory. Our academic environment encourages critical thinking, collaboration, and entrepreneurial spirit.
Scholarships & Financial Support
Making Quality Education Accessible
KIET is deeply committed to supporting deserving and talented students through its scholarship and financial aid programs. Various financial support options are available, including need-based assistance, merit-based scholarships, and discounts for siblings and alumni. Students facing financial challenges are encouraged to apply early and discuss available support with the admissions team. Our goal is to ensure that financial limitations never stand in the way of quality education. Every year, hundreds of KIET students benefit from these programs and graduate with pride and dignity.
Investing in Your Potential
We believe that financial limitations should never stand in the way of talent and ambition.
Flexible Scholarship Options
Merit-based, need-based, and special category scholarships are available for eligible students.
Supporting Every Background
From sibling discounts to aid for differently-abled students, KIET supports diverse financial needs.
Apply Early, Maximize Benefits
Early applicants receive priority consideration for financial assistance and scholarship programs.
Campus Experience
A Place That Inspires Growth
The main campus, located at Ahsanabad, is a purpose-built facility with modern architecture and serene surroundings. Classrooms are equipped with multimedia tools and air-conditioning, while specialized labs serve engineering, computing, and aviation disciplines. The campus features an extensive digital library, cafeterias, student lounges, and sports facilities to support student well-being. Students can also engage in clubs, competitions, and cultural events that bring campus life alive. The environment at KIET encourages curiosity, confidence, and a strong sense of community among its students.
Why KIET?
A University That Cares About Your Future
Students at KIET benefit from a strong academic foundation combined with career development and global exposure. Our curriculum is reviewed by industry leaders to ensure it remains relevant and impactful. Internship opportunities, corporate visits, seminars, and job placement support are integral parts of student life. We are proud to have a growing network of alumni working in top national and international organizations. At KIET, you are not just a student — you are part of a lifelong learning journey guided by mentorship and purpose.
How to Apply
A Simple and Streamlined Process
Applying to KIET is quick and easy through our online admissions portal. Start by creating an account, fill in the application form, and upload your documents. Once the application is submitted, our team will guide you through the next steps, including the entry test or interview if required. Applicants are encouraged to submit their applications early to take advantage of available scholarships and priority processing. Support is available through phone, WhatsApp, and email in case you have any questions during the process.

ORIC-KIET
Driving Innovation and Research
Welcome to ORIC at KIET — where knowledge meets impact. Driving innovation, supporting research, and building bridges between academia and industry.
Explore More
About ORIC-KIET
About the Office of Research, Innovation & Commercialization (ORIC)
The Office of Research, Innovation & Commercialization (ORIC) at KIET fosters a dynamic ecosystem for high-impact research, innovation, and industry partnerships.
KIET, chartered by the Government of Sindh in 2000, serves over 4,000 students and 9,000+ alumni through its three colleges: Engineering, Management Sciences, and Computing & College of Computing and Information Systems.
ORIC is building a vibrant ecosystem to transform research into real-world impact.
A Thriving Research Environment
With a team of 27+ PhD-qualified faculty members and growing research collaborations, KIET’s research culture continues to flourish. Numerous research projects have recently been funded by the Higher Education Commission (HEC), reinforcing our national relevance.
Our programs
Management
Research in the College of Management Sciences emphasizes real-world business innovation through areas like Marketing, Finance, Human Resource Management, and Supply Chain Management, contributing to both academic and industry growth.
Computer Science
The College of Computing and Information Systems focuses on emerging technologies such as Cloud & Grid Computing, Data Science, Machine Learning, Cybersecurity, Software Engineering, and the Internet of Things (IoT), addressing modern digital challenges.
Engineering
KIET’s College of Engineering conducts cutting-edge research in Intelligent Robotics, Embedded Systems, Signal Processing, Smart Networks, Electronics, and Aerodynamics. These areas are driven by advanced technologies and led by experienced PhD faculty
KIET’s vision for research & innovation
KIET is establishing its ORIC at a transformative point in its R&D journey. Having already built a strong foundation in research and development, we now focus on identifying and eliminating barriers that hinder innovation and research commercialization.
Advancing Research Support
ORIC at KIET is dedicated to strengthening the university’s R&D by streamlining access to research grants and ensuring ethical research practices.
Innovation & Entrepreneurship
We empower faculty and students to lead innovative projects while encouraging a startup culture through incubation and entrepreneurial support.
Industry Links
ORIC bridges the gap between academia and industry by facilitating patents, partnerships, and the commercialization of research outcomes.
Our Mission & Vision
Mission
To strengthen the research culture at KIET by encouraging innovation, commercialization, and collaborations that serve national development.
Vision
To be recognized as a hub of impactful research, cutting-edge innovation, and academic-industry synergy in Pakistan and beyond.

Market Forces

Directorate of Advanced Studies & Research (DASR)
With a mission to be a research-led university, KIET has a rapidly developing and vibrant research ecosystem consisting of world-class researchers, facilities, stakeholder engagement, and administrative support system.
Explore
Read More
Our Mission & Vision
Elevating postgraduate studies through quality, innovation, and impact.
Established in Spring 2025, DASR serves as the central academic and administrative body overseeing all MS and PhD programs at KIET. We coordinate research, supervise academic standards, and promote multidisciplinary collaborations to position KIET as a hub for research excellence in Pakistan.
LATEST NEWS
PhD final defense of our scholar Mr. Mohammad Ayub Latif is scheduled to be held on 6 November 2025 at 11:00 AM in the Conference Room, NN Campus, or via Zoom.
PhD final defense of our scholar Mr. Zubair is scheduled to be held 25 September 2025 at 10 am NN campus.
Renaming of GSSE to DASR
Announcement of an Open Ph.D. Defense of Zeba Shariff Khan on July 26 2023
Announcement of an Open PhD Defense of Mr. Zubair Ahmed Shah on September 30, 2021.
Graduate Programs Supervision
Elevating postgraduate studies through quality, innovation, and impact.
DASR ensures quality in curricula, assessments, and research across all faculties. From course planning to thesis defenses, we provide transparent, well-structured academic pathways, enabling students to achieve their academic and professional goals.
Driving Knowledge Beyond Boundaries
Collaborative research for a better future.
We promote high-impact interdisciplinary research across fields like AI, sustainable development, healthcare, and cybersecurity. Our scholars and faculty are encouraged to publish, innovate, and contribute to national and international research landscapes.
Research Enterprise
KIET culture of collaboration drives innovative discoveries in areas vital to our world, our health and our intellectual life.
Interdisciplinary Research
At the intersection of disciplines is where new ideas emerge and innovative research happens.
Institutes, Labs & Centers
Independent labs, centers and institutes engage faculty and students from across the university.
Other research centers & labs
Academic departments sponsor numerous other research centers and labs.
Research Conferences
KIET organize individual research conferences specifically from Engineering, Management & Computing Sciences departments.
ICCIS-CoCIS
International Conference on Computing & Information Sciences
To establish the research culture among the students and faculty members, KIET sort out a global gathering, International Conference on Computing & Information Sciences (ICCIS). To produce quality professionals by using diverse learning methodology, aspiring faculty, innovative curriculum and cutting edge research, in the field of computing & information sciences
INTELLECT-CoE
International Conference On Electrical Engineering
The aim of this conference is to bring together researchers, scientists, academicians and representatives of industry to discuss the latest directions of research in engineering technologies, identify solutions to current national problems through industry-academia collaboration and to contribute in nation building.
IRCBM-CoMS
International Research Conferences on Business Management
The aim of IRCBM is to provide platform for educators, Researcher, Academicians, Scholars, Managers, Graduate students and Entrepreneurs from different culture backgrounds to present and discuss researches, development and innovation in the field of Business Management and Entrepreneurship.
Director’s Message
Inspiring research, fostering excellence.
At DASR, research is more than an academic requirement—it’s our commitment to society. Together, let’s advance knowledge, uphold integrity, and shape a future defined by innovation.” — Prof. Dr. Maaz Bin Ahmad
Read More
News & updates
Stay informed with the latest announcements, events, and opportunities from DASR KIET.
Connect with DASR
Partner with us in shaping the research leaders of tomorrow.
Whether you’re a prospective student, faculty member, or research partner, DASR welcomes collaboration and dialogue. Contact us to learn more about our programs, research support, and academic initiatives.

Introduction
Introduction
Karachi Institute of Economics & Technology  is a degree awarding institution imparting quality education in the field of computing & information sciences, management sciences, engineering and media & arts.
College of Computing and information sciences  at KIET is working with a clear vision of producing high quality technology entrepreneurs & market leaders for national & International market. To establish a culture of research and innovation, CoCIS has taken two major steps:
Organizes a biannual conference, International Conference of Computing and Information Sciences , available at www.iccis.pafkiet.edu.pk
Publishes a biannual peer reviewed journal, KIET Journal of Computing & Information Sciences , available at www.kjcis.pafkiet.edu.pk.
ICCIS aims to provide a forum to present new research problems and recent results of the emerging technologies in the field of computing and information sciences. The conference also endeavors to act as a bridge between academia and industry and to encourage fruitful collaboration. It brings together researchers, faculty members, consultants, industry experts and technologist on one platform.
No registration fee for authors and participants to ease the impact of COVID-19 pandemic.
"This is a hybrid mode conference (Online+Physical)"
About KIET
About KIET
Vision Statement
"To be amongst the leading universities of the world where an excellent environment for education, research and innovation exists, to produce quality leaders."
Mission Statement
"KIET is committed in providing a quality learning and research environment in order to inculcate fundamental and specialized knowledge and skills for producing competent professionals with focus on technology, innovation and entrepreneurship."
VISIT
KARACHI INSTITUTE OF ECONOMICS & TECHNOLOGY
About CoCIS
About CoCIS
Vision Statement
"To develop technology entrepreneurs & leaders for national & international market."
Mission Statement
"To produce quality professionals by using diverse learning methodology, aspiring faculty, innovative curriculum and cutting edge research, in the field of computing & information sciences."

Submissions GuideLines
Submissions GuideLines
For submission of manuscripts please click on
EasyChair
All paper submissions will be managed electronically, so you may only submit complete and properly formatted paper. Paper length must be 4-6 pages (minimum-maximum limit). Any incomplete submission will be rejected.
For submission of manuscript follow the steps below:
Select your area of interest from technical tracks
Complete your Manuscript in
IEEE Format
Submit via
EasyChair
Technical Tracks
ICCIS 2021
, is a multi-track conference and invites original unpublished manuscripts for submission in the following technical tracks:
Pattern Classification
"Pattern Recognition, Multidimensional Signal Processing, Image Processing, Biomedical, Genome Signal Processing, Neuromorphic Computing, Bioinformatics, Compressive Sensing Convolutional, Neural Networks, Speech/Music Processing and Coding, Deep Learning and Machine Learning"
Computing Applications
"Cloud/Fog Computing, Information Security, Mobile Computing, Operating Systems, Data Mining, Management Information Systems, Computing Architectures, Machine Learning, Super Computing, Information Theory, Coding Memory Systems, Software Engineering, Grid Computing, Cluster Computing, Database technologies & its application, Internet of things (IoT)"
Big Data Analytics
"Data Science, Data Analytics, Big Data Analytics, Feature Engineering, Data Mining, Data Warehousing and Data Visualization"
Communication and Networking
"Internet Of Things, Cellular and Broadband Wireless, Green Communication, Mobile Ad hoc Networks, Cognitive Radios Software, Defined Networks, Peer to Peer Networks, Next Generation Networks, Quality of Service, Data Management, 5G & Beyond Technologies, Optical & RF Networks, Peer to Peer Networks, Wireless Sensor Networks, LiFi Communication, Secure Communication Networks, Cyber Physical Systems"
Proceedings of ICCIS 2021
All accepted and presented papers at ICCIS 2021 will be submitted to IEEE Xplore (Digital Library) for conference proceedings publication.
Previous ICCIS Proceedings are
available here
Plagiarism policy
Policy on Multiple Submissions and Prior Publication:
A submission must present original work which has not appeared/accepted elsewhere for publication, or has not been concurrently submitted to another conference/journal publication. All submitted papers will undergo plagiarism checks in multiple plagiarism detection software as mandated by IEEE. Papers found violating the above mentioned plagiarism policy may be rejected at any stage: before review, after review, after acceptance notification or before submission of conference proceedings for publishing in IEEE Xplore. For specific IEEE policy on plagiarism please visit:
https://www.ieee.org/publications/rights/copyright-faqs.html
https://www.ieee.org/publications/rights/plagiarism/id-plagiarism.html

4TH INTERNATIONAL CONFERENCE OF COMPUTING & INFORMATION SCIENCES

CALL FOR PAPER

College of Computing and Information Sciences, Karachi Institute of Economics and Technology is organizing a two
day conference: 4th International Conference on Computing & Information Sciences (ICCIS 2021) Latest trends in Com-
puting Technologies on 29-30 November 2021 in Karachi.This conference is technically co-sponsored by IEEE Karachi
Section and will be addressing the latest research directions in the field of Pattern Classification, Computing Applica-
tions, Big Data Analytics, Communication and Networks. This conference aims to bring together researchers, scien-
tists, academicians, and representatives of industry to discuss the latest directions of research in engineering technol-
ogies, identify solutions to current national problems through industry-academia collaboration, and to contribute in
nation-building.

About the Faculty of Computer Science
The Faculty of Computer Science at KIET offers a dynamic learning environment focused on innovation, research, and real-world impact. With a strong foundation in theory and hands-on practice, we prepare students for the future of technology through state-of-the-art labs, industry-driven curriculum, and experienced faculty.
Academic Programs
BS Computer Science
Accredited by NCEAC, designed with market-driven skills
MS Computer Science
Research-focused with specializations in AI, Networks, and Data Science
PhD Computer Science
Advanced research for future academicians and scientists
Message
Welcome to the Faculty of Computing & Information Sciences (FoCIS) at KIET. As President of this esteemed institution, I am proud to lead a dynamic academic environment where innovation, research, and excellence converge. At CoCIS, we are committed to equipping our students with the knowledge, skills, and ethical grounding necessary to thrive in the rapidly evolving digital world. With a focus on emerging technologies such as Artificial Intelligence, Cybersecurity, Data Science, and Software Engineering, our programs are designed to meet global standards while addressing local challenges. As a PhD scholar and lifelong advocate for quality education, I believe in nurturing not just competent professionals, but visionary leaders who will shape the future of technology in Pakistan and beyond. I invite you to explore the opportunities that await you at FoCIS and become a part of our journey towards academic and technological excellence.
Dr. Khalid Khan
President, KIET
Meet Our Faculty
Our faculty includes PhD scholars and industry veterans with expertise in Machine Learning, Data Analytics, Cloud Computing, and more. Their mentorship transforms students into professionals ready to lead.
The strength of the Faculty of Computing & Information Sciences (FoCIS) lies in its distinguished and diverse faculty. Our team comprises experienced professors, PhD scholars, and industry professionals dedicated to academic excellence, cutting-edge research, and student mentorship. With expertise in fields such as Artificial Intelligence, Cybersecurity, Cloud Computing, Software Engineering, and Data Science, our faculty members bring real-world relevance to the classroom and foster a culture of innovation, ethics, and collaboration.
“At FoCIS, we’re not just teaching the tools of today — we’re inspiring students to build the technology of tomorrow.”
Permanent Faculty
Adjunct Faculty
